CORPORATE PROFILE
企業簡介
Established in 1991, Luk Fook Holdings (International)
Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively
referred to as the “Group”) is one of the leading jewellery
retailers in Hong Kong and Mainland. The Group principally
engages in the sourcing, designing, wholesaling, trademark
licensing and retailing of a variety of gold and platinum jewellery
and gem-set jewellery products, with a total of over 2,100 points
of sale in 10 countries and regions across Hong Kong, Macau,
Mainland, Singapore, Malaysia, Cambodia, the Philippines, the
United States, Canada and Australia.

The Company was listed on the Main Board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited in May 1997.

六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本
集團」）成立於1991年，是香港及內地主要珠寶零售商之一。本
集團主要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品之採購、設計、
批發、商標授權及零售業務。集團現時在香港、澳門、內地、新加
坡、馬來西亞、柬埔寨、菲律賓、美國、加拿大及澳洲共10個國家
和地區共有逾2,100個零售點。

本公司於1997年5月在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。


CORPORATE VISION
企業願景
The corporate vision of the Group is to be the “Brand of Hong
Kong, Sparkling the World”. This is also our core brand value. The
Group always puts forth maximum effort for “Lukfook” to reach its
goal of becoming a globally recognised and desired brand, and
as one of the Hong Kong’s most renowned brands in the luxury
industry, the Group commits to crafting the finest jewellery for
customers to further establish its reputation in the international
market.

「香港名牌•國際演繹」為本集團的企業願景及品牌核心價值。
 本集團一直堅持不懈，盡心竭力使「六福」成為全球認知及喜愛
 的品牌。作為香港知名奢侈品牌，本集團致力為全球顧客提供優
 質首飾，以進一步確立其於國際市場上的聲譽。




2       Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited 六福集團（國際）有限公司

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告
Dear Shareholders,                                                  各位股東：

On behalf of Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited (the          本人謹代表六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公
“Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the     司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」或「我們」）
“Group” or “We”), I hereby present the Group’s annual results        謹 此 提 呈 本 集 團 截 至2020年3月31日 止 年 度
for the year ended 31 March 2020 (“FY2020” or the “Year under       （「2020財年」或「回顧年度」）之全年業績。
review”).

During the Year under review, with the continuing impact of US-     回顧年度內，受中美貿易戰、香港社會事件及
China trade war, social activities in Hong Kong and COVID-19        2019冠狀病毒病疫情嚴峻的持續影響，本集團
pandemic, both the Group’s revenue and profit attributable to       於2020財年的收入及全年的權益持有人應佔溢
equity holders for FY2020 were unsatisfactory.                      利皆未如理想。

The Group’s total revenue for the Year under review amounted to     本集團於回顧年度的總收入為11,233,771,000
HK$11,233,771,000 (2019: HK$15,859,990,000), representing           港 元（2019年：15,859,990,000港 元），較 去
a decrease of 29.2% over the corresponding period of last year.     年 同 期 減 少29.2%。權 益 持 有 人 應 佔 溢 利
Profit attributable to equity holders decreased by 42.0% to         則 減 少42.0%至866,315,000港 元（2019年：
HK$866,315,000 (2019: HK$1,492,747,000), with earnings per          1,492,747,000港 元），每 股 盈 利 為1.48港 元

In response to the COVID-19 pandemic in 2020, the Group             就2020年的新型冠狀病毒疫情，本集團捐贈人
donated RMB2 million to support hospitals in Wuhan to combat        民幣200萬元以助武漢醫院支援抗疫一線，同
against the COVID-19 pandemic on the frontline. We have also        時，我們已即時於所有店舖採取了以下應對措
immediately taken the following measures for all our shops to       施，以保障客人健康及權益：
safeguard the health and interests of our customers:

•    Employees                                                      •   員工方面
     Employees are required to measure body temperature every           每天須量度體溫，及經常使用消毒搓手液
     day, clean their hands with alcohol-based handrub in a             清潔雙手；任何時候均需要配戴外科手術
     frequent manner, wear surgical masks at all times, carry out       口罩；全面加強員工個人衛生，及嚴格執
     enhanced personal hygiene measures, and strictly follow            行消毒清潔指引。
     disinfection and cleaning instructions.

•    Facilities                                                     •   設施方面
     Cleaning and disinfection work in the shops are                    加強店內的清潔及消毒工作，每2至3小時
     strengthened and carried out every 2 to 3 hours; shops are         進行清潔及消毒；配合當地政府或商場的
     closed or business hours are shortened pursuant to the             要求，停止對外營業或縮短營業時間。
     requirements of the local government or shopping malls.

•    Sales services                                                 •   銷售服務方面
     The sales staff will help customers to measure body                銷售員工會協助客人量度體溫及使用消
     temperature, and clean their hands with alcohol-based              毒搓手液清潔雙手；所有飾物會於客人試
     handrub. All jewellery will be disinfected before and after        戴前及後進行消毒。
     fitting of customers.

•    Reward points of members                                       •   會員尊享積分方面
     To protect the rights and interests of members, their reward       疫情期間，為保障會員權益，積分或會籍
     points or membership which expires during the period from          於2020年1月 至3月 期 間 失 效 之 會 員，其
     January to March 2020 are extended for 3 months during             有效期均延期3個月，讓各位會員有更多
     the pandemic, so that they will have more time to process          時間處理積分及享受會員禮遇。

 檢驗                production, release of final product to delivery of products to retail shops, in compliance with
                   the Product Quality Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Law of the People’s Republic
                   of China on the Protection of Consumer Rights and Interests, the Trade Descriptions Ordinance
                   of Hong Kong and other applicable regulations on product quality standards. We also conduct
                   quality inspections when raw materials are delivered and when finished products are shipped.
                   Our inspection center has been awarded the ISO/IEC17025:2005 International Test/Calibration
                   Laboratory Certification.
                   按照《中華人民共和國產品質量法》、                      《中華人民共和國消費者權益保護法》、香港《商品說明
                   條例》等有關產品質量標準的適用法規，本集團已於原材料來貨、工藝製作、製成品出貨、零
                   售店抵運等流程執行全方位質量檢驗。原材料來貨時及製成品出貨時會進行質量檢測，該檢
                   驗中心已獲發ISO/IEC17025:2005國際測試╱校正實驗室認證。

 After-sales       We are committed to providing comprehensive after-sales services to customer after our
 Services          products are sold, to ensure the quality of products after delivery. We have drawn up the
 售後服務              Products Recall and Complaint Handling Procedures. All quality issues of products identified by
                   customers will be handled and followed up by relevant retail shops. After initial inspection by the
                   retail shops, the products will be handed over to the inspection center for further inspection, to

                   產品售出後，我們仍承諾為顧客提供完善的售後服務，確保產品交付後的質量。我們已制訂產
                   品回收及投訴處理流程，如顧客發現產品有任何質量問題，可交予零售店作跟進處理。零售店
                   會先進行初步檢測，再交由鑑定中心作檢驗並進行後續跟進及處理，確保顧客滿意產品質量。

wastewater and electroplating wastewater, as well as, domestic                       廢水、電金廢水等生產污水以及生活污水。為
sewage in the course of its production and daily operations. To                      免未經處理的污水對周邊環境造成污染，工廠
prevent unprocessed sewage from polluting the surrounding                            區已設置污水處理設施，包括石膏廢水一體化
environment, we have set up sewage treatment facilities in                           處理系統、中水回用系統和地下生化池，將石
the plant area, including an integrated treatment system for                         膏廢水、電金廢水、濃水和生活污水進行預處
gypsum wastewater, a water reuse system and an underground                           理，方可對外排放，以滿足《水污染物排放限
biochemical pool. Our gypsum wastewater, electroplating                              值》（DB44/26-2001）第二段一級標準。此外，
wastewater, concentrated water and domestic sewage are                               我們每星期會檢查工廠的雨水溝渠和洗手間，
pretreated before discharging, to meet the second-level                              防止有人將不達標的生產污水倒入並污染附近
standard of the second range of the Water Pollutant Discharge                        水源。
Limit (DB44/26-2001). Also, we inspect the rainwater ditches
and toilets of the factory every week, to prevent people from
discharging production sewage that falls short of relevant
standards and polluting nearby water sources.

“Hiking × Eco Clean up” Action of the Lukfook Volunteer Team
六福義工隊「行山×執垃圾」行動

                                                The country parks in Hong Kong are full of trash left behind by
                                                visitors, which not only damages the scenery, but also brings
                                                environmental hygiene issues, and increases the risk of accidental
                                                ingestion of trash by wild animals that affects the ecosystem. In view
                                                of this, Lukfook Volunteer Team joined hands with Green Power to
                                                organize “Hiking × Eco Clean up” Action to carry baskets on their
                                                backs and clean up refuse along the trails in country parks, working
                                                together to protect our environment.
                                                香港郊野公園滿佈遊人遺下垃圾，不但破壞景觀，亦會帶來環境衛
                                                生問題，同時增加野生動物誤食垃圾的風險，影響生態環境。有見及
                                                此，六福義工隊與綠色力量舉辦「行山×執垃圾」行動，身體力行背
                                                上清潔籃，協助清理郊野公園路徑垃圾，共同努力保護我們的環境。


Lukfook Volunteer Team visited people with mental disabilities
六福義工隊探訪智力發展有障礙人士

To promote the integration of the disabled and able-bodied, Lukfook
Volunteer Team partnered with Fu Hong Society to visit mentally
challenged people and make “fluid painting” with them, hoping to
support their rehabilitation and development through cultural and
artistic activities, and enable them to realise their potential. Artistic
creation can also promote their communication with volunteers, and
help them to integrate into society.
為了實踐促進傷健共融，六福義工隊與扶康會合作，探訪智力發展
障礙人士，並與他們一同製作「流體畫」                        ，希望透過文化藝術活動，
協助他們身心康復和發展，讓他們盡展所能。藝術創作亦可促進他
們與義工的溝通，幫助他們融入社會。


Lukfook Volunteer Team visited the elderly
六福義工隊長者探訪

                                                                       Lukfook Volunteer Team partnered with the
                                                                       Hong Kong Christian Service to organise an
                                                                       elderly visit campaign. Our volunteers had tea
                                                                       with senior friends, to extend care and warmth
                                                                       to the elderly.
                                                                       六福義工隊與香港基督教服務處合作，舉辦長
                                                                       者探訪義工活動，與長者共晉下午茶，為長者
                                                                       帶來溫暖和關懷。

Recognised as “Happy Company” for 6 Consecutive Years
連續六年獲認可為「開心企業」

 The Group has been recognised as “Happy Company” in the “Happiness-at-work Promotional Scheme” for
 six consecutive years. The Happiness-at-work Promotional Scheme is hosted by the Promoting Happiness
 Index Foundation of Hong Kong and implemented by the Hong Kong Productivity Council, aiming to raise the
 recognition of Hong Kong companies and organizations for “Happiness-at-work” and advance the creation of a
 happy working environment by all sectors of society, thereby raising the happiness-at-work level of Hong Kong
 people.
 本集團連續六年在「開心工作間」推廣計劃中獲認可為「開心企業」                                        。「開心工作間」推廣計劃由香港提升快
 樂指數基金主辦，並由香港生產力促進局執行，宗旨在於提升香港企業及機構對「開心工作間」的認同感，
 促進各界攜手建設愉快的工作環境，共同提高香港人的工作快樂水平。

  During the Reporting Period, the Group was named one of the “Best Participating Company in Online Learning
  Program” on the “E-learning Platform” organised by Hong Kong Jewellers’ & Goldsmiths’ Association (the
  “HKJGA”) and Mr. WONG Chit, a frontline employee of the Group, was honoured as one of the “The Best
  Performer” for scoring a success in test results on the “E-learning Platform”. HKJGA, Trade and Industry
  Department and all players have made dedicated efforts to promote the scheme and roll out the “Intelligent
  Learning Platform” for the retail jewellery industry, to explore innovative training strategies and development.
  This platform helps reinforce the jewellery knowledge of frontline employees in the jewellery industry and their
  adaptability during operation. We hope that more learning and training opportunities will be provided to enhance
  the overall competitiveness of practitioners and facilitate the long-term development of the jewellery industry.
  The “Intelligent Learning Platform” is a project implemented by the HK Association and sponsored by the Trade
  and Industry Department. The platform develops training videos on basic jewellery knowledge and business
  regulations, as well as, an electronic problem base which can assess the professional knowledge of retail
  jewellery practitioners. The Group hopes that the service standard and professional image of our frontline staff
  can be raised via participating in the project, to further enhance the industry reputation of the Group.
  於報告期間，本集團於香港珠石玉器金銀首飾業商會（「香港商會」）主辦之「智能學習平台」測驗，榮膺「最
  踴躍參與電子課程機構」之一，而本集團前線員工王捷先生亦成為「最佳網上測試表現從業員」之一。是次
  計劃有賴香港商會、工業貿易署及各同業的努力推動，推出針對珠寶零售業的「智能學習平台」                                                            ，開拓嶄新的
  培訓策略發展。這平台有助加強珠寶行業前線員工的珠寶知識及營運時的應變能力，我們期望未來會有更
  多的學習及培訓機會，提高從業員整體競爭力，並促進珠寶行業的長遠發展。                                                 「智能學習平台」是由香港商會
  推行，並獲得工業貿易署撥款支持的項目。該平台具備關於珠寶基礎知識及營商法規條例的培訓短片和電
  子問題庫，可評估珠寶零售從業員的專業知識。本集團希望可藉參與是次項目加強前線員工服務水平及專
  業形象，以進一步提升本集團在業界的聲譽。

環境、社會及管治報告

The Group made a concerted effort to combat the pandemic
集團上下攜手對抗疫情

 In response to the outbreak of COVID-19, the Group is well-positioned to prevent and control the pandemic, and
 safeguard the health and safety of our employees. The jewellery processing plant in Nansha implemented the
 measures of quarantine inspection, health protection and safety production upon the resumption of operation
 in compliance with the “Work Program for On-site Inspection of Industrial Enterprises upon Resumption of
 Work” issued by the local government. We provided online training on “Knowledge of Preventing and Controlling
 COVID-19” and distributed “Personal Hygiene Handbook for COVID-19” to our employees, to communicate
 on the working area and knowledge of personal and household protection, instruct employees to wear masks,
 check body temperature, wash hand frequently, disinfect regularly, reduce meetings and maintain social distance,
 as well as, minimize the risk of spreading the virus in the park and among employees.
 因應2019冠狀病毒病的爆發，本集團全體上下均嚴陣以待，做好防疫抗疫工作，保障員工健康與安全。位於
 南沙的珠寶加工廠按照地區政府發出的《工業企業復工現場核查工作方案》，於工廠復工時做好檢疫查驗、
 健康保護和安全生產措施。我們向員工提供「新型冠狀病毒肺炎個人防控知識」線上培訓，並派發《新型冠
 狀病毒肺炎個人防護手冊》，講解工作區域、個人和居家防護知識，指導員工上班佩戴口罩、接受體溫檢測、
 勤洗手、定期消毒、減少會議並保持社交距離，盡最大程度減低病毒於園區內和員工之間傳播的風險。

The Board of the Company recognises that sound and                 本公司的董事會深明，在強調完整性、透明度、
effective corporate governance practices and procedures,           問責性及獨立性之情況下，穩健及有效之企業
with an emphasis on integrity, transparency, accountability and    管治常規及程序對提升股東價值及保障股東權
independence, are essential to enhance the shareholders’ value     益極為重要。本公司承諾維持良好企業管治標
and safeguard the shareholders’ interests. The Company is          準，並致力確保按照所有適用規則及規例進行
committed to maintaining a good corporate governance standard      業務。
and endeavours to ensure that its businesses are conducted in
accordance with all applicable rules and regulations.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                        企業管治常規
The Company has applied the principles and complied with all          於回顧年度內，本公司已採用聯交所證券上市
code provisions and, where applicable, the recommended best           規則（「上市規則」）附錄14所載之《企業管治守
practices of the Corporate Governance Code and Corporate              則》及《企業管治報告》 （「企業管治守則」）的
Governance Report (the “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to        原則，並已遵守所有守則條文及建議最佳常規
the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities (the “Listing Rules”)   （如適用），惟下列偏離者除外：
on Stock Exchange during the Year under review, except for the
following deviation:

Code Provision A.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman              守則條文第A.2.1條規定主席與行政總裁的角色
and chief executive should be separate and should not be              應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任，使董事會
performed by the same individual, so that there is a clear division   管理與本集團日常業務管理之職責清楚劃分，
of responsibilities for the management of the Board and the           以確保權力和授權之平衡。
day-to-day management of the Group’s business to ensure a
balance of power and authority.

In view of the increasing trend of business deriving from the         由於內地市場帶動業務增長及基於其對「對等
Mainland market, it is believed that Mr. WONG Wai Sheung              階級」之觀念，相信由黃偉常先生擔任本公司
being the Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company         主席兼行政總裁，可望於未來在內地進行商務
will further enhance the business development of the Group in         磋商時，進一步提升本集團在內地市場的業務
the Mainland market due to its norms on “status parity” when          發展。此外，董事會成員亦包括合資格專業人
future business negotiations are conducted in Mainland. Besides,      士及社會上其他顯赫、富經驗之個別人士。董
members of the Board also include qualified professionals and         事會認為，於董事會轄下委員會及兩名副主席
other prominent and experienced individuals from our community.       之協助下，現有董事會之組成可確保權力和授
The Board considers that the existing Board composition, with         權之平衡。儘管如此，董事會將不時檢討此架
the support of Board Committees and two Deputy Chairmen,              構，並於適當時候，考慮將兩職分開。
can ensure a balance of power and authority. The Board will
nevertheless review this structure from time to time and will
consider the segregation of the two roles at the appropriate time.

INTERNAL AUDIT                                                          內部審核
The internal audit is an independent function undertaken by             內部審核為內部審計部負責的獨立工作，並分
the Internal Audit Department reporting administratively to the         別向本公司管理層及審核委員會作出行政及
Company’s management and reporting functionally to the Audit            功能匯報。內部審計部獲授權取得一切所需信
Committee. The Internal Audit Department is authorised to obtain        息，以完成內部審核工作。
all information necessary to accomplish internal audit work.

The Board has conducted an annual review to ensure the                  董事會已作年度檢討以確保本公司在會計、內
adequacy of resources, staff qualifications and experience,             部審核及財務匯報職能方面的資源、員工資歷
training programmes and budget of the Company’s accounting,             及經驗、培訓課程及有關預算均屬足夠。
internal audit and financial reporting functions.

A risk-based audit approach has been adopted by the Internal            內部審計部採用風險主導審核方針，定期檢討
Audit Department. Internal control reviews are conducted regularly      內部監控，密切監察業務或營運變動，定期進
to closely monitor business or operation changes. Regular audit         行審核測試，以確保主要監控正常運作。內部
tests are carried out to ensure key controls are in operation.          審計部亦視乎需要進行其他項目及調查工作。
The Internal Audit Department also conducts other projects and
investigating work as required.

Any significant internal audit findings are discussed with respective   任何重大內部審核結果均與各有關部門主管予
department heads to have actions agreed and subsequently                以討論，議定行動並作出跟進，以確保維持滿
followed up, in order to ensure that satisfactory control is            意的監控。

Rent                                                                   租金
As a retailer, rent accounts for a considerable portion of the         作為零售商，租金佔本集團的開支比重不少，
Group’s expenses. Rental increment on retail shops directly            零售商舖的租金上升直接影響集團之盈利。我
affects the profit of the Group. The Group strikes a good balance      們在店舖擴張與增加盈利之間取得一個良好平
between shop expansion and profit increment. The Group strives         衡。本集團積極透過與業主商討減租、關閉盈
to lower rent via negotiations with landlords, closes branches with    利能力較低的店舖，並於其他地段尋求合適位
low profitability and searches for suitable locations in other areas   置開設新店。
for new shops.

EMOLUMENT POLICY                                                         薪酬政策
The emolument policy regarding the employees of the Group                本集團僱員之薪酬政策乃基於僱員之表現、資
is based on their merit, qualifications and competence. The              歷及能力。董事之薪酬由薪酬委員會經參考本
emoluments of the directors are reviewed and determined by               公司之營運業績、個別僱員表現及可資比較市
the Remuneration Committee, having regard to the Company’s               場數據而檢討及釐定。概無任何董事或其任何
operating results, individual performance and comparable market          聯繫人士參與釐定其本身之薪酬。
statistics. No director, or any of his or her associates involved in
deciding his or her own remuneration.

1     GENERAL INFORMATION                                              1   一般資料
      Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited (the “Company”)            六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」）於
      was incorporated in Bermuda on 3 September 1996 as                   1996年9月3日在百慕達根據百慕達公司
      a company with limited liability under the Companies Act             法註冊成立為有限公司，其註冊辦事處地
      of Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Victoria         址為Victoria Place, 5th Floor, 31 Victoria
      Place, 5th Floor, 31 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM 10,                Street, Hamilton HM 10, Bermuda。
      Bermuda.

      The Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”)             本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主
      are principally engaged in the sourcing, designing,                  要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品
      wholesaling, trademark licensing and retailing of a variety of       之採購、設計、批發、商標授權及零售業
      gold and platinum jewellery, and gem-set jewellery products.         務。

      The Company’s shares were listed on the main board of                本公司股份於1997年5月6日在香港聯合
      The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                  交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
      Exchange”) on 6 May 1997.

      These consolidated financial statements are presented in             除另有說明者外，此等綜合財務報表均
      Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”), unless otherwise stated, and              以港元（「港元」）呈列，並已於2020年6月
      have been approved for issue by the board of directors on            26日獲董事會批准刊發。
      26 June 2020.

2     BASIS OF PREPARATION AND CHANGE IN                               2   編製基準及會計政策變動
      ACCOUNTING POLICIES
      The principal accounting policies applied in the preparation         編製此等綜合財務報表時所應用之主要
      of these consolidated financial statements are set out               會計政策載於下文。除另有說明者外，此
      below. These policies have been consistently applied to all          等政策已於所有呈報年度貫徹應用。
      the years presented, unless otherwise stated.

      2.1 Basis of preparation                                             2.1 編製基準
            The consolidated financial statements of the Company                本公司之綜合財務報表乃根據香港
            have been prepared in accordance with Hong Kong                     財 務 報 告 準 則（「香 港 財 務 報 告 準
            Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRS”) and the                     則」）及香港公司條例第622章之披
            disclosure requirements of Hong Kong Companies                      露規定編製。除如附註4所解釋，按
            Ordinance Cap.622. The consolidated financial                       公允值計入其他全面收入之金融資
            statements have been prepared under the historical                  產、黃金借貸及衍生金融工具為按
            cost convention except that financial assets at fair                公允值列賬外，綜合財務報表按歷
            value through other comprehensive income, gold                      史成本慣例編製。
            loans and derivatives financial instruments are carried
            at fair values, as explained in Note 4.

            The preparation of consolidated financial statements                編製與香港財務報告準則貫徹一致
            in conformity with HKFRS requires the use of                        之綜合財務報表需要使用若干關鍵
            certain critical accounting estimates. It also requires             會計估算，同時亦需要管理層在應
            management to exercise its judgement in the process                 用本集團會計政策時作出判斷。涉
            of applying the Group’s accounting policies. The areas              及高度判斷或高度複雜性的範疇，
            involving a higher degree of judgement or complexity,               或涉及對綜合財務報表屬重大假設
            or areas where assumptions and estimates are                        和估算的範疇，在附註5中披露。
            significant to the consolidated financial statements are
            disclosed in Note 5.

        transitional provisions in the standard using a modified          經修訂追溯採納法而並無重列2019

      POLICIES
      3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                             3.1 合併原則及權益會計處理
          accounting
          (a)   Business combinations                                          (a)   業務合併
                The acquisition method of accounting is used                         本集團採用購買會計法將所
                to account for all business combinations,                            有業務合併入賬，不論是否已
                regardless of whether equity instruments or                          購買權益工具或其他資產。購
                other assets are acquired. The consideration                         買一間附屬公司轉讓的代價
                transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                      包括：
                comprises the:

                (i)     fair values of the assets transferred                        (i)     所轉讓資產的公允值

                (ii)    liabilities incurred to the former owners of                 (ii)    被收購業務之前擁有人
                        the acquired business                                                所產生負債

                (iii)   equity interests issued by the Group                         (iii)   本集團已發行股本權益

                (iv)    fair value of any asset or liability resulting               (iv)    或有代價安排所產生任
                        from a contingent consideration                                      何資產或負債的公允
                        arrangement, and                                                     值，及

                (v)     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest               (v)     附屬公司任何先前存在
                        in the subsidiary.                                                   的股本權益的公允值。

                Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and                     在業務合併中所購買的可識
                contingent liabilities assumed in a business                         別資產以及所承擔的負債及
                combination are, with limited exceptions,                            或然負債，初步以其於購買日
                measured initially at their fair values at the                       期的公允值計量（少數例外情
                acquisition date. The Group recognises any                           況除外）。本集團以逐項購買
                non-controlling interest in the acquired entity                      基準，按公允值或按非控股權
                on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either                        益所佔被收購實體可識別資
                at fair value or at the non-controlling interest’s                   產淨值的比例確認於被收購
                proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net                     實體的任何非控股權益。
                identifiable assets.

                Acquisition-related costs are expensed as                            購買相關成本於產生時支銷。
                incurred.

          (b)   Subsidiaries                                                 (b)   附屬公司
                Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                附屬公司指本集團擁有控制
                entities) over which the Group has control. The                    權的所有實體（包括結構性實
                Group controls an entity when the Group is                         體）。當本集團自參與實體業
                exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                     務而承擔取得其可變回報的風
                from its involvement with the entity and has the                   險或享有可變回報的權利，並
                ability to affect those returns through its power                  有能力透過其對實體活動的
                to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries               主導權影響該等回報時，則本
                are fully consolidated from the date on which                      集團控制該實體。附屬公司於
                control is transferred to the Group. They are                      控制權轉移至本集團當日起
                deconsolidated from the date that control                          全面合併入賬，並於終止控制
                ceases.                                                            權當日起不再合併入賬。

                Intercompany transactions, balances and                            集團公司間交易、結存及未變
                unrealised gains on transactions between group                     現收益均會對銷。未變現虧損
                companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                    亦會對銷，除非該交易顯示已
                also eliminated unless the transaction provides                    轉讓資產減值的證據。附屬公

      3.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                             3.9 非金融資產之減值
          Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or         沒有確定可使用年期之無形資產或
          intangible assets not ready to use are not subject to            未達到可使用狀態的無形資產毋須
          amortisation and are tested annually for impairment.             攤銷，並每年進行減值檢測。須作
          Assets that are subject to amortisation are reviewed             攤銷之資產乃於發生任何事件或環
          for impairment whenever events or changes in                     境變化顯示其賬面值可能不能收回
          circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may              時作減值檢討。倘資產之賬面值超
          not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised             過其可收回金額，會就其差額確認
          for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount              減值虧損。資產之可收回金額為其
          exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable                  公允值減出售成本與其可使用價值
          amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs         中較高者。就評估減值而言，資產乃
          to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing          按最低水平之可獨立識別現金流量
          impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels              （現金產生單位）予以分類。出現減
          for which there are separately identifiable cash                 值之非金融資產（商譽除外）將於每
          flows (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets              個報告日期審閱是否有撥回減值之
          other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are              可能。
          reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at
          each reporting date.

          first-out basis. The cost of finished goods comprises             品成本包括原材料、直接勞工成本

          It excludes borrowing costs. Net realisable value is              本。可變現淨值乃按於日常業務過
          the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of             程中之估計售價減適用之可變銷售

    3.18 Current and deferred income tax                                   3.18 即期及遞延所得稅
        The tax expense for the period comprises current and                  期內稅項開支包括即期及遞延稅
        deferred tax. Tax is recognised in the consolidated                   項。稅項於綜合損益表確認，惟倘稅
        income statement, except to the extent that it relates                項與於其他全面收入確認或直接於
        to items recognised in other comprehensive income or                  權益確認之項目有關者除外。在該
        directly in equity. In this case the tax is also recognised           情況下，稅項亦會分別於其他全面
        in other comprehensive income or directly in equity,                  收入或直接於權益內確認。
        respectively.

        (a)   Current income tax                                              (a)   即期所得稅
              The current income tax charge is calculated                           即期所得稅開支乃按結算日在
              on the basis of the tax laws enacted or                               本公司之附屬公司及聯營公司
              substantively enacted at the balance sheet                            經營及產生應課稅收入之國家
              date in the countries where the Company’s                             已實行或大致已實行之稅法計
              subsidiaries and associates operate and                               算。管理層定期評估就適用稅
              generate taxable income. Management                                   項法規有待詮釋之情況之稅項
              periodically evaluates positions taken in                             申報所採取措施，並在適當情
              tax returns with respect to situations in                             況下按預期將支付予稅務機
              which applicable tax regulation is subject to                         構之款項為基準計提撥備。
              interpretation. It establishes provisions where
              appropriate on the basis of amounts expected
              to be paid to the tax authorities.

        (b)   Deferred income tax                                             (b)   遞延所得稅
              Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the                    遞延所得稅利用負債法就資產
              liability method, on temporary differences arising                    和負債之稅基與在綜合財務
              between the tax bases of assets and liabilities                       報表之賬面值產生之暫時差
              and their carrying amounts in the consolidated                        額悉數計提撥備。然而，若遞
              financial statements. However, deferred tax                           延稅項負債來自商譽之初步
              liabilities are not recognised if they arise from                     確認，則不予確認。若遞延所
              the initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred                         得稅來自在交易（不包括業務
              income tax is also not accounted for if it arises                     合併）中對資產或負債之初步
              from initial recognition of an asset or liability in a                確認，而在交易時不影響會計
              transaction other than a business combination                         損益或應課稅溢利或虧損，則
              that at the time of the transaction affects neither                   亦不作記賬。遞延所得稅採用
              accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                       在報告期末前已頒佈或實質
              income tax is determined using tax rates (and                         頒佈，並在有關之遞延所得稅
              laws) that have been enacted or substantially                         資產實現或遞延所得稅負債

              are expected to apply when the related deferred                       法例）而釐定。
              income tax asset is realised or the deferred
              income tax liability is settled.

    3.21 Leases                                                          3.21 租賃
        As explained in Note 2.2 above, the Group has                       誠如上文附註2.2所闡述，本集團已

        Group is the lessee. The new policy is described                    計政策作出變動。新政策載述於下
        below and the impact of the change in Note 2.2.                     文，而變動之影響載述於附註2.2

        Until the year ended 31 March 2019, leases of                       直 至 截 至2019年3月31日 止 年 度，
        property, plant and equipment were classified as                    物業、廠房及設備之租賃被分類為
        operating leases. Payments made under operating                     經營租賃。根據經營租賃支付之款
        leases (net of any incentives received from the lessor)             項（扣除已收出租人之任何優惠）以
        were charged to profit or loss on a straight-line basis             直線法於租賃期內自損益扣除。
        over the period of the lease.

        From 1 April 2019, leases are recognised as a right-                自2019年4月1日起，租賃在租賃資
        of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the date              產可供本集團使用之日確認為使用
        at which the leased asset is available for use by the               權資產，並確認相應負債。每筆租賃
        Group. Each lease payment is allocated between the                  付款於負債及財務費用間分配。財
        liability and finance cost. The finance cost is charged             務費用於租賃期內自損益扣除，以
        to profit or loss over the lease period so as to produce            計算出各期間負債結餘之固定週期
        a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining               利率。使用權資產乃按資產可使用
        balance of the liability for each period. The right-of-use          年期或租賃期（以較短者為準）以直
        asset is depreciated over the shorter of the asset’s                線法折舊。
        useful life and the lease term on a straight-line basis.

                   •    Trade receivables                                               •   貿易應收賬項

                   The Group applies the HKFRS 9 simplified                             本集團應用香港財務報告準則
                   approach to measure expected credit losses                           第9號簡化方法計量預期信貸
                   which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance                        虧損，該方法就所有貿易應收
                   for all trade receivables.                                           賬項採用全期預期虧損撥備。

                   To measure expected credit losses, the Group                         為計量預期信貸虧損，本集團
                   categorises its trade receivables based in the                       根據客戶賬戶性質及攤佔信
                   nature of customer accounts and shared credit                        貸風險特徵，將其貿易應收賬
                   risk characteristics.                                                項分類。

                   The expected loss rates are based on the                             預期虧損率乃分別基於2020
                   payment profiles of sales over a period of 24                        年3月31日 或2019年3月31日
                   month before 31 March 2020 or 31 March 2019                          前24個月期間之銷售付款記
                   respectively and the corresponding historical                        錄及於有關期間內已經歷之相
                   credit losses experienced within the respective                      應過往信貸虧損得出。過往虧
                   period. The historical loss rates are adjusted to                    損率乃經調整以反映影響客戶
                   reflect current and forward-looking information                      結算應收賬項能力之宏觀經濟
                   on macroeconomic factors affecting the ability of                    因素的當前及前瞻性信息。
                   the customers to settle the receivables.

    (b)   Fair value estimation                                             (b)   公允值估計
          The carrying amounts of the Group’s financial assets,                   本集團金融資產（包括現金及銀行
          including cash and bank balances, trade receivables,                    結餘、貿易應收賬項、其他應收賬

          and bank borrowings approximate their fair values due                   賬項及銀行貸款）因各自的期限，其
          to their maturities.                                                    賬面值與公允值相若。

          The fair values of financial instruments traded in active               於活躍市場買賣的金融工具（如黃
          markets (such as gold loans) are based on quoted                        金借貸）的公允值，按於結算日的市
          market prices at the balance sheet date. The quoted                     場報價計算。就本集團所持金融資
          market price used for financial assets held by the                      產採用的市場報價為當前買入價。
          Group is the current bid price.

          The fair values of financial assets and liabilities not                 未於活躍市場買賣的金融資產及負
          traded in active markets are estimated by discounting                   債之公允值乃按本集團就類似金融
          the future contractual cash flows at the current market                 工具可獲得之現時市場利率貼現未
          interest rate that is available to the Group for similar                來合約現金流量而估計。
          financial instruments.

          The table below analyses financial instruments carried                  下表為採用估值法對按公允值列賬

          have been defined as follows:                                           級別如下：

          •     Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for                  •   同類資產或負債在活躍市場

                                                                                       。

                within level 1 that are observable for the asset                      產或負債的可直接（即價格）

      (c)   Capital risk management                                        (c)   資本風險管理
            The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                  本集團管理資本的目標為保障本集
            safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going                 團持續運作的能力，從而為股東提
            concern in order to provide returns for shareholders                 供回報、為其他利益相關者提供利
            and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an               益以及保持理想的資本架構以減低
            optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.             資本成本。

            The Group manages the capital structure and makes                    本集團管理資本架構，並因應經濟
            adjustments to it in the light of changes in economic                環境的變動作出調整。為保持或調
            conditions. In order to maintain or adjust the capital               整資本架構，本集團可調整支付予
            structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                         股東的股息、發行新股或獲得新的
            payments to shareholders, issue of new shares or                     銀行貸款。
            obtain new bank borrowings.

            The Group also monitors capital on the basis of                      本集團亦以負債資產比率監察其資
            gearing ratio. The gearing ratio is calculated as net                本。負債資產比率是以淨（現金）╱
            (cash)/debt divided by total capital. Net (cash)/debt                債項除以總資本計算。淨（現金）╱
            is calculated as total borrowings (including bank                    債項是以貸款總額（包括銀行貸款
            borrowings and gold loans) less cash and bank                        及黃金借貸）減現金及銀行結餘計
            balances. Total capital is calculated as “equity”, as                算得出。總資本乃以「權益」（如綜
            shown in the consolidated balance sheet, plus net                    合資產負債表所示）加淨（現金）╱
            (cash)/debt.                                                         債項計算得出。

      The contributed surplus of the Company represents the              本公司之實繳盈餘指本公司為交換Luk
      difference between the nominal value of the shares issued          Fook Investment (B.V.I.) Limited全部已發
      by the Company in exchange for all the issued ordinary             行普通股而發行股份之面值與本公司於
      shares of Luk Fook Investment (B.V.I.) Limited and the value       1997年本集團重組期間所收購相關附屬
      of net assets of the underlying subsidiaries acquired by           公司之資產淨值兩者之差額。根據百慕達
      the Company during the Group’s reorganisation in 1997.             《1981年公司法》       （經修訂）        ，實繳盈餘
      Under the Companies Act 1981 of Bermuda (as amended),              可派發予股東，惟本公司自實繳盈餘派付
      the contributed surplus is distributable to the shareholders,      股息後，須能於負債到期時支付債務；或
      provided that the Company would, after the payment of              本公司之資產可變現值據此不會少於其
      dividends out of the contributed surplus, be able to pay           債務、其已發行股本及其股份溢價之總
      its liabilities as they become due; or the realisable value        和。就本集團層面而言，此實繳盈餘重新
      of the Company’s assets would thereby not be less than             分類為各相關附屬公司之儲備部分。
      the aggregate of its liabilities, its issued share capital and
      its share premium. At the Group level, the contributed
      surplus is reclassified into its component of reserves of the
      underlying subsidiaries.
